> 10	#   verifica se o processo exigido (`.require`, com `<slug>` substituído) existe no disco.
> 11	#
> 12	#   O que ele NÃO faz, e é honesto dizer: não lê o conteúdo do processo. Um arquivo
> 13	#   vazio em `docs/processes/<slug>.md` passa neste gate. Ele prova EXISTÊNCIA, não
> 14	#   qualidade — Operador/Sequência/Verificação/Recuperação/Sucesso são julgamento humano.
> 15	#
> 16	# USAGE
> 17	#   sop-gate.sh              # modo diff: julga os arquivos novos
> 18	#   sop-gate.sh --explain    # mostra o mapeamento, o que casou e o que falta, sem julgar
> 19	#   sop-gate.sh --selftest   # prova que o gate fica vermelho (Lei 1)
> 20	#
> 21	# EXIT CODES (contrato firme)
> 22	#   0  nenhuma pendência — ou `law2_process` desligado, ou `sop_map` vazio (com aviso
> 23	#      explícito em stderr: verde por ausência de regra, não por aprovação)
> 24	#   1  falta ao menos um processo exigido por um mapeamento que casou
> 25	#   3  erro de configuração / uso — lib ausente, `sop_map` ilegível, fora de repositório git
> 26	#   `--explain` e `--list` não julgam (0). `--selftest` sai 1 se algum caso falhar.
> 27	#
> 28	# DEPENDÊNCIAS
> 29	#   bash, coreutils, git, grep -E, sed. Nada mais é assumido.
> 30	
> 31	set -euo pipefail
> 32	
> 33	# ── localização e contrato da biblioteca compartilhada ────────────────────────
> 34	SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd -P)"
> 35	SELF="$SCRIPT_DIR/$(basename -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")"
> 36	HARNESS_DIR="$(cd -- "$SCRIPT_DIR/.." && pwd -P)"
> 37	LIB="$HARNESS_DIR/lib/harness-lib.sh"
> 38	
> 39	if [ ! -r "$LIB" ]; then
> 40	  printf 'sop-gate: biblioteca ausente ou ilegível em %s — instalação incompleta.\n' "$LIB" >&2
> 41	  exit 3
> 42	fi
> 43	# shellcheck source=../lib/harness-lib.sh
> 44	. "$LIB"
> 45	
> 46	for _fn in cfg cfg_list have say warn fail ok repo_root die; do
> 47	  if ! declare -F "$_fn" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
> 48	    printf 'sop-gate: a lib %s não expõe a função obrigatória `%s` — contrato quebrado.\n' \
> 49	      "$LIB" "$_fn" >&2
> 50	    exit 3
> 51	  fi
> 52	done
> 53	unset _fn
> 54	
> 55	_say()  { say  "$@" || true; }
> 56	_warn() { warn "$@" || true; }
> 57	_ok()   { ok   "$@" || true; }
> 58	_fail() { fail "$@" || true; }
> 59	
> 60	usage() { sed -n '3,30p' "$SELF" | sed -e 's/^# \{0,1\}//'; }
> 61	
> 62	# ── configuração ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 63	ROOT="$(repo_root)"
> 64	LAW2_ENABLED="$(cfg law2_process.enabled true)"
> 65	PROCESSES_REL="$(cfg paths.processes docs/processes)"
> 66	
> 67	# Usado APENAS como sonda de consistência (ver check_sop_map_readable): a leitura
> 68	# canônica de configuração é sempre pela lib.
> 69	config_file_guess() {
> 70	  local c
> 71	  for c in "${HARNESS_CONFIG:-}" "$HARNESS_DIR/harness.config.json" \
> 72	           "$ROOT/.harness/harness.config.json"; do
> 73	    [ -n "$c" ] || continue
> 74	    if [ -r "$c" ]; then printf '%s' "$c"; return 0; fi
> 75	  done
> 76	  return 1
> 77	}
> 78	
> 79	# `sop_map` é um array de OBJETOS. `cfg_list` pode devolvê-lo compacto (uma linha por
> 80	# objeto) ou expandido (várias linhas): normalizamos para um fluxo único e fatiamos por
> 81	# `}`, extraindo `when`/`require` dentro de cada registro — assim a ordem das chaves
> 82	# dentro do objeto não importa.
> 83	read_sop_map() {
> 84	  local blob rec when req
> 85	  blob="$(cfg_list law2_process.sop_map 2>/dev/null | tr '\n' ' ' || true)"
> 86	  [ -n "${blob// /}" ] || return 0
> 87	  printf '%s' "$blob" | tr '}' '\n' | while IFS= read -r rec; do
> 88	    case "$rec" in *'"when"'*) ;; *) continue ;; esac
> 89	    when="$(printf '%s' "$rec" \
> 90	      | grep -oE '"when"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*"[^"]*"' | head -n 1 \
> 91	      | sed -E 's/^"when"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*"//; s/"$//' || true)"
> 92	    req="$(printf '%s' "$rec" \
> 93	      | grep -oE '"require"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*"[^"]*"' | head -n 1 \
> 94	      | sed -E 's/^"require"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*"//; s/"$//' || true)"
> 95	    [ -n "$when" ] || continue
> 96	    if [ -z "$req" ]; then
> 97	      printf 'sop-gate: mapeamento com `when=%s` sem `require` — ignorado.\n' "$when" >&2
> 98	      continue
> 99	    fi
> 100	    printf '%s\t%s\n' "$when" "$req"
> 101	  done
> 102	}
> 103	
> 104	# Falso-verde é pior que vermelho: se o arquivo de configuração contém mapeamentos mas
> 105	# a leitura devolveu zero, o gate é INCONCLUSIVO e sai 3 em vez de fingir "sem regra".
> 106	check_sop_map_readable() {
> 107	  local n="$1" conf
> 108	  [ "$n" -eq 0 ] || return 0
> 109	  conf="$(config_file_guess || true)"
> 110	  [ -n "$conf" ] || return 0
> 111	  if grep -qE '"sop_map"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*\[[[:space:]]*\{' "$conf" 2>/dev/null \
> 112	     || grep -qE '"when"[[:space:]]*:' "$conf" 2>/dev/null; then
> 113	    printf 'sop-gate: `law2_process.sop_map` tem entradas em %s, mas a leitura via lib\n' "$conf" >&2
> 114	    printf '          devolveu ZERO mapeamentos. Gate INCONCLUSIVO — não vou reportar verde.\n' >&2
> 115	    return 1
> 116	  fi
> 117	  return 0
> 118	}
> 119	
> 120	# ── casamento de glob (idêntico ao spec-gate: mesma semântica, mesmo resultado) ─
> 121	glob_match() {
> 122	  local path="$1" pat="$2" rest base
> 123	  # shellcheck disable=SC2053
> 124	  [[ "$path" == $pat ]] && return 0
> 125	  if [ "${pat:0:3}" = '**/' ]; then
> 126	    rest="${pat:3}"
> 127	    # shellcheck disable=SC2053
> 128	    [[ "$path" == $rest ]] && return 0
> 129	  fi
> 130	  if [ "${#pat}" -gt 3 ] && [ "${pat: -3}" = '/**' ]; then
> 131	    base="${pat:0:${#pat}-3}"
> 132	    [ "$path" = "$base" ] && return 0
> 133	    case "$path" in "$base"/*) return 0 ;; esac
> 134	  fi
> 135	  return 1
> 136	}
> 137	
> 138	static_prefix() {
> 139	  local pat="$1" out="" seg old_ifs segs
> 140	  old_ifs="${IFS-}"
> 141	  IFS='/'
> 142	  read -r -a segs <<< "$pat"
> 143	  IFS="$old_ifs"
> 144	  for seg in "${segs[@]}"; do
> 145	    case "$seg" in *[*?[]*) break ;; esac
> 146	    out="${out}${seg}/"
> 147	  done
> 148	  printf '%s' "$out"
> 149	}
> 150	
> 151	slugify() {
> 152	  printf '%s' "$1" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]' \
> 153	    | sed -E 's/[^a-z0-9]+/-/g; s/^-+//; s/-+$//'
> 154	}
> 155	
> 156	derive_slug() {
> 157	  local path="$1" pat="$2" prefix rel first
> 158	  prefix="$(static_prefix "$pat")"
> 159	  if [ -n "$prefix" ] && [ "${path#"$prefix"}" != "$path" ]; then
> 160	    rel="${path#"$prefix"}"
> 161	  else
> 162	    rel="$(basename -- "$(dirname -- "$path")")"
> 163	    [ "$rel" = "." ] && rel="$(basename -- "$path")"
> 164	  fi
> 165	  first="${rel%%/*}"
> 166	  first="${first%.*}"
> 167	  slugify "$first"
> 168	}
> 169	
> 170	resolve_require() {
> 171	  local template="$1" slug="$2" out
> 172	  out="${template//<slug>/$slug}"
> 173	  case "$out" in
> 174	    /*) printf '%s' "$out" ;;
> 175	    *)  printf '%s/%s' "$ROOT" "$out" ;;
> 176	  esac
> 177	}
> 178	
> 179	# ── coleta: apenas ARQUIVOS NOVOS ─────────────────────────────────────────────
> 180	in_git_repo() { git -C "$ROOT" rev-parse --is-inside-work-tree >/dev/null 2>&1; }
> 181	
> 182	collect_new_files() {
> 183	  {
> 184	    if git -C "$ROOT" rev-parse --verify -q HEAD >/dev/null 2>&1; then
> 185	      git -C "$ROOT" diff --name-only --diff-filter=A HEAD 2>/dev/null || true
> 186	    fi
> 187	    git -C "$ROOT" diff --name-only --diff-filter=A --cached 2>/dev/null || true
> 188	    git -C "$ROOT" ls-files --others --exclude-standard 2>/dev/null || true
> 189	  } | sed '/^[[:space:]]*$/d' | sort -u
> 190	}
> 191	
> 192	# ── comandos ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 193	load_map() {
> 194	  MAP_WHEN=()
> 195	  MAP_REQ=()
> 196	  local line
> 197	  while IFS= read -r line; do
> 198	    [ -n "$line" ] || continue
> 199	    MAP_WHEN+=("$(printf '%s' "$line" | cut -f1)")
> 200	    MAP_REQ+=("$(printf '%s' "$line" | cut -f2)")
> 201	  done < <(read_sop_map)
> 202	}
> 203	
> 204	cmd_explain() {
> 205	  local i
> 206	  printf '── sop-gate --explain ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
> 207	  printf 'raiz do repositório : %s\n' "$ROOT"
> 208	  printf 'law2_process.enabled: %s\n' "$LAW2_ENABLED"
> 209	  printf 'paths.processes     : %s\n' "$PROCESSES_REL"
> 210	  printf 'mapeamentos (%s):\n' "${#MAP_WHEN[@]}"
> 211	  if [ "${#MAP_WHEN[@]}" -eq 0 ]; then
> 212	    printf '  <nenhum> — Lei 2 sem gate mecânico neste projeto; só doutrina.\n'
> 213	  else
> 214	    for i in "${!MAP_WHEN[@]}"; do
> 215	      printf '  when=%-28s require=%s\n' "${MAP_WHEN[$i]}" "${MAP_REQ[$i]}"
> 216	    done
> 217	  fi
> 218	  printf '\n'
> 219	  if ! in_git_repo; then
> 220	    printf 'Fora de um repositório git — nada a inspecionar.\n'
> 221	    printf '\n(--explain não julga: nenhum veredito emitido.)\n'
> 222	    return 0
> 223	  fi
> 224	  printf 'arquivos NOVOS (adicionados no índice + não rastreados):\n'
> 225	  local path slug req hit
> 226	  while IFS= read -r path; do
> 227	    [ -n "$path" ] || continue
> 228	    hit=0
> 229	    for i in "${!MAP_WHEN[@]}"; do
> 230	      if glob_match "$path" "${MAP_WHEN[$i]}"; then
> 231	        hit=1
> 232	        slug="$(derive_slug "$path" "${MAP_WHEN[$i]}")"
> 233	        req="$(resolve_require "${MAP_REQ[$i]}" "$slug")"
> 234	        if [ -f "$req" ]; then
> 235	          printf '  [ok]     %s → slug `%s` → %s (existe)\n' "$path" "$slug" "${req#"$ROOT"/}"
> 236	        else
> 237	          printf '  [FALTA]  %s → slug `%s` → %s (ausente)\n' "$path" "$slug" "${req#"$ROOT"/}"
> 238	        fi
> 239	      fi
> 240	    done
> 241	    if [ "$hit" -eq 0 ]; then printf '  [neutro] %s\n' "$path"; fi
> 242	  done < <(collect_new_files)
> 243	  printf '\n(--explain não julga: nenhum veredito emitido.)\n'
> 244	  return 0
> 245	}
> 246	
> 247	cmd_diff() {
> 248	  if [ "$LAW2_ENABLED" = "false" ]; then
> 249	    printf 'sop-gate: DESLIGADO (law2_process.enabled=false) — nenhum veredito emitido.\n' >&2
> 250	    return 0
> 251	  fi
> 252	  if [ "${#MAP_WHEN[@]}" -eq 0 ]; then
> 253	    printf 'Lei 2 sem gate mecânico neste projeto — apenas doutrina.\n' >&2
> 254	    printf '  (`law2_process.sop_map` está vazio: este verde é ausência de regra, não aprovação.\n' >&2
> 255	    printf '   Preencha o mapa na Fase D se quiser que a Lei 2 tenha exit code.)\n' >&2
> 256	    return 0
> 257	  fi
> 258	  if ! in_git_repo; then
> 259	    printf 'sop-gate: o modo diff exige um repositório git (raiz testada: %s).\n' "$ROOT" >&2
> 260	    return 3
> 261	  fi
> 262	
> 263	  local path i slug req rc=0 checked=0
> 264	  local -a missing_pairs=()
> 265	  while IFS= read -r path; do
> 266	    [ -n "$path" ] || continue
> 267	    for i in "${!MAP_WHEN[@]}"; do
> 268	      if glob_match "$path" "${MAP_WHEN[$i]}"; then
> 269	        checked=$((checked + 1))
> 270	        slug="$(derive_slug "$path" "${MAP_WHEN[$i]}")"
> 271	        if [ -z "$slug" ]; then
> 272	          _warn "arquivo novo $path casou '${MAP_WHEN[$i]}' mas não produziu slug — ignorado"
> 273	          continue
> 274	        fi
> 275	        req="$(resolve_require "${MAP_REQ[$i]}" "$slug")"
> 276	        if [ -f "$req" ]; then
> 277	          _ok "$path → processo presente: ${req#"$ROOT"/}"
> 278	        else
> 279	          _fail "$path → processo AUSENTE: ${req#"$ROOT"/}"
> 280	          missing_pairs+=("$path"$'\t'"$slug"$'\t'"${req#"$ROOT"/}")
> 281	          rc=1
> 282	        fi
> 283	      fi
> 284	    done
> 285	  done < <(collect_new_files)
> 286	
> 287	  if [ "$checked" -eq 0 ]; then
> 288	    _ok 'nenhum arquivo novo casou os mapeamentos da Lei 2'
> 289	    return 0
> 290	  fi
> 291	
> 292	  if [ "$rc" -ne 0 ]; then
> 293	    local pair p s r
> 294	    {
> 295	      printf '\n'
> 296	      printf 'Lei 2 (Processo Antecipado) bloqueia esta mudança.\n'
> 297	      printf '\n'
> 298	      printf 'Especificamente: há código novo sem o processo humano equivalente documentado.\n'
> 299	      printf '\n'
> 300	      for pair in "${missing_pairs[@]}"; do
> 301	        p="$(printf '%s' "$pair" | cut -f1)"
> 302	        s="$(printf '%s' "$pair" | cut -f2)"
> 303	        r="$(printf '%s' "$pair" | cut -f3)"
> 304	        printf '  %s  →  falta `%s`  (slug `%s`)\n' "$p" "$r" "$s"
> 305	      done
> 306	      printf '\n'
> 307	      printf 'Preciso do processo escrito antes de prosseguir — Operador / Sequência /\n'
> 308	      printf 'Gates de verificação / Caminho de recuperação / Sinal de sucesso.\n'
> 309	      printf '\n'
> 310	      printf 'Alternativa segura: escrevo o processo primeiro e só então o código.\n'
> 311	      printf '\n'
> 312	    } >&2
> 313	  fi
> 314	  return "$rc"
> 315	}
> 316	
> 317	# ── selftest ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 318	selftest_case() {
> 319	  local tmp="$1" desc="$2" expected="$3"; shift 3
> 320	  local rc=0 out="$tmp/.selftest-out"
> 321	  if [ "$#" -gt 0 ]; then
> 322	    ( cd "$tmp" && HARNESS_CONFIG="$tmp/.harness/harness.config.json" \
> 323	        bash .harness/bin/sop-gate.sh "$@" ) >"$out" 2>&1 || rc=$?
> 324	  else
> 325	    ( cd "$tmp" && HARNESS_CONFIG="$tmp/.harness/harness.config.json" \
> 326	        bash .harness/bin/sop-gate.sh ) >"$out" 2>&1 || rc=$?
> 327	  fi
> 328	  if [ "$rc" -eq "$expected" ]; then
> 329	    printf '  [PASS] exit %s (esperado %s) — %s\n' "$rc" "$expected" "$desc"
> 330	    return 0
> 331	  fi
> 332	  printf '  [FAIL] exit %s (esperado %s) — %s\n' "$rc" "$expected" "$desc"
> 333	  sed -e 's/^/         | /' "$out"
> 334	  return 1
> 335	}
> 336	
> 337	write_conf() {
> 338	  local tmp="$1" sop_map="$2"
> 339	  cat > "$tmp/.harness/harness.config.json" <<JSON
> 340	{
> 341	  "kit_version": "1.0.0",
> 342	  "project": { "name": "selftest", "slug": "selftest", "response_language": "pt-BR", "code_language": "en" },
> 343	  "roles": { "operator": "AI Agent", "reviewer": "selftest", "owner": "selftest", "escalation": "" },
> 344	  "paths": {
> 345	    "specs": "specs",
> 346	    "processes": "docs/processes",
> 347	    "architecture": "docs/architecture",
> 348	    "handoff": "HANDOFF.md",
> 349	    "archive": "docs/handoff-archive",
> 350	    "knowledge": "docs/knowledge"
> 351	  },
> 352	  "spec_gate": { "enabled": true, "required": ["00-index"], "optional": [], "gate_marker_regex": "<!--\\\\s*GATE:\\\\s*(draft|review|open|sealed)\\\\s*-->", "trigger_paths": [], "exempt_patterns": [] },
> 353	  "law2_process": {
> 354	    "enabled": true,
> 355	    "sop_map": $sop_map
> 356	  },
> 357	  "knowledge": { "backend": "file", "sink": "docs/knowledge/observations.md" }
> 358	}
> 359	JSON
> 360	}
> 361	
> 362	# O trap de EXIT roda DEPOIS que o escopo da função morreu: a variável precisa ser
> 363	# global, senão `set -u` mata a limpeza e ainda contamina o exit code do script.
> 364	SELFTEST_TMP=''
> 365	selftest_cleanup() {
> 366	  if [ -n "${SELFTEST_TMP:-}" ] && [ -d "${SELFTEST_TMP:-}" ]; then
> 367	    rm -rf "$SELFTEST_TMP"
> 368	  fi
> 369	  return 0
> 370	}
> 371	
> 372	cmd_selftest() {
> 373	  local tmp fails=0
> 374	  SELFTEST_TMP="$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/harness-sop-gate-selftest.XXXXXX")"
> 375	  tmp="$SELFTEST_TMP"
> 376	  trap selftest_cleanup EXIT
> 377	
> 378	  mkdir -p "$tmp/.harness/bin" "$tmp/.harness/lib" "$tmp/docs/processes" "$tmp/src/integrations"
> 379	  cp -- "$LIB" "$tmp/.harness/lib/harness-lib.sh"
> 380	  cp -- "$SELF" "$tmp/.harness/bin/sop-gate.sh"
> 381	  write_conf "$tmp" '[{ "when": "src/integrations/**", "require": "docs/processes/<slug>.md" }]'
> 382	  printf '# baseline\n' > "$tmp/README.md"
> 383	
> 384	  (
> 385	    cd "$tmp"
> 386	    git init -q . >/dev/null 2>&1
> 387	    git -c user.email=selftest@harness -c user.name=selftest add -A >/dev/null 2>&1
> 388	    git -c user.email=selftest@harness -c user.name=selftest commit -q -m baseline >/dev/null 2>&1
> 389	  ) || true
> 390	
> 391	  printf 'sop-gate --selftest · árvore sintética em %s\n\n' "$tmp"
> 392	
> 393	  if [ ! -d "$tmp/.git" ]; then
> 394	    printf '  [FAIL] git indisponível — o selftest não consegue exercitar o modo diff\n' >&2
> 395	    return 1
> 396	  fi
> 397	
> 398	  printf 'A. mapeamento ativo\n'
> 399	  selftest_case "$tmp" 'sem arquivo novo → nada a julgar' 0 || fails=$((fails + 1))
> 400	
> 401	  mkdir -p "$tmp/src/integrations/pagamentos"
> 402	  printf 'const c = 1;\n' > "$tmp/src/integrations/pagamentos/client.ts"
> 403	  selftest_case "$tmp" 'integração nova SEM processo → bloqueia' 1 || fails=$((fails + 1))
> 404	
> 405	  printf '# Processo: pagamentos\n' > "$tmp/docs/processes/pagamentos.md"
> 406	  selftest_case "$tmp" 'mesmo arquivo COM processo → libera' 0 || fails=$((fails + 1))
> 407	
> 408	  printf 'const z = 2;\n' > "$tmp/src/nao-mapeado.ts"
> 409	  selftest_case "$tmp" 'arquivo fora do mapeamento é neutro' 0 || fails=$((fails + 1))
> 410	  rm -f "$tmp/src/nao-mapeado.ts"
> 411	
> 412	  printf '\nB. mapa vazio (honestidade explícita em vez de silêncio verde)\n'
> 413	  write_conf "$tmp" '[]'
> 414	  local out="$tmp/.selftest-empty"
> 415	  local rc=0
> 416	  ( cd "$tmp" && HARNESS_CONFIG="$tmp/.harness/harness.config.json" \
> 417	      bash .harness/bin/sop-gate.sh ) >"$out" 2>&1 || rc=$?
> 418	  if [ "$rc" -eq 0 ] && grep -qE 'apenas doutrina' "$out"; then
> 419	    printf '  [PASS] exit 0 + aviso "Lei 2 sem gate mecânico … apenas doutrina" em stderr\n'
> 420	  else
> 421	    printf '  [FAIL] exit %s e/ou aviso ausente com sop_map vazio\n' "$rc"
> 422	    sed -e 's/^/         | /' "$out"
> 423	    fails=$((fails + 1))
> 424	  fi
> 425	
> 426	  printf '\nC. modos informativos\n'
> 427	  write_conf "$tmp" '[{ "when": "src/integrations/**", "require": "docs/processes/<slug>.md" }]'
> 428	  selftest_case "$tmp" '--explain não julga (com pendência no disco)' 0 --explain || fails=$((fails + 1))
> 429	  selftest_case "$tmp" 'argumento desconhecido → erro de uso' 3 --nao-existe || fails=$((fails + 1))
> 430	
> 431	  printf '\n'
> 432	  if [ "$fails" -eq 0 ]; then
> 433	    printf 'selftest: TODOS OS CASOS PASSARAM — o gate foi visto vermelho e verde.\n'
> 434	    return 0
> 435	  fi
> 436	  printf 'selftest: %s CASO(S) FALHARAM — o gate não é confiável neste ambiente.\n' "$fails" >&2
> 437	  return 1
> 438	}
> 439	
> 440	# ── despacho ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 441	MODE=diff
> 442	while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
> 443	  case "$1" in
> 444	    --explain)  MODE=explain ;;
> 445	    --selftest) MODE=selftest ;;
> 446	    -h|--help)  usage; exit 0 ;;
> 447	    *)
> 448	      printf 'sop-gate: argumento desconhecido: %s\n\n' "$1" >&2
> 449	      usage >&2
> 450	      exit 3 ;;
> 451	  esac
> 452	  shift
> 453	done
> 454	
> 455	RC=0
> 456	if [ "$MODE" = "selftest" ]; then
> 457	  cmd_selftest || RC=$?
> 458	  exit "$RC"
> 459	fi
> 460	
> 461	MAP_WHEN=()
> 462	MAP_REQ=()
> 463	load_map

> ```
> 1	# Harness Kit v1.0.0
> 2	
> 3	Kit de engenharia e arquitetura de software para projetos conduzidos com agentes de IA.
> 4	Descompacte na raiz de qualquer repositório e peça ao agente para configurar a base.
> 5	
> 6	## Uso
> 7	
> 8	```bash
> 9	unzip harness-kit-v1.0.0.zip -d harness-kit-tmp
> 10	```
> 11	
> 12	Depois, na sessão com o agente:
> 13	
> 14	> "Preciso iniciar um novo desenvolvimento. Configure toda a base com o kit em `harness-kit-tmp/`."
> 15	
> 16	O agente lê `START-HERE.md`, executa o ritual de instalação em 5 fases (reconhecimento →
> 17	entrevista → instalação → adaptação → prova de ingestão) e deixa o repositório com o harness
> 18	ativo. A entrevista da Fase B é obrigatória: é ela que ensina ao harness quais comandos
> 19	provam sanidade **neste** stack.
> 20	
> 21	## O que ele instala
> 22	
> 23	| Camada | Onde | O que faz |
> 24	|---|---|---|
> 25	| Leis | `.claude/rules/00-laws.md` | Materialidade · Processo Antecipado · Poda · ORO |
> 26	| Spec Gate | `.claude/rules/01-closed-loop.md` + `.harness/bin/spec-gate.sh` | Impede código de módulo novo sem especificação revisada |
> 27	| Governança | `.claude/rules/02-governance.md` | Donos de documentação e regra de escalação |
> 28	| Rituais | `.claude/skills/` | Pega e selo de sessão, auditoria, descoberta de automação |
> 29	| Curadores | `.claude/agents/` | Subagentes donos de specs, docs e plano de ação |
> 30	| Gates executáveis | `.harness/bin/` | Exit code, não prosa interpretada |
> 31	| Moldes | `.harness/templates/` | Suíte de spec 00→09, SOP, ADR, HANDOFF |
> 32	
> 33	## Requisitos
> 34	
> 35	`bash`, `git`, `grep`, `sed`, `awk`, `coreutils`. Tudo o mais é detectado e opcional.
> 36	Nenhuma linguagem, framework ou serviço é assumido.
> 37	
> 38	## Princípio
> 39	
> 40	O kit não sabe o que você está construindo. Ele impõe **como** se constrói: com prova
> 41	material, processo antes de automação, especificação antes de código, e alguém nomeado
> 42	como dono do prejuízo.
> 43	
> 44	O que ele **não** garante está escrito com todas as letras em `START-HERE.md` §3 — inclusive
> 45	que a doutrina entrar no contexto não significa que ela será obedecida. Os gates que precisam
> 46	sobreviver à ausência de supervisão são scripts com `exit 1`, não parágrafos.
> 47	
> 48	---
> 49	
> 50	Licença: use, modifique e redistribua livremente.
> 51	
> ```

}\n\n# Emite `path<TAB>linha<TAB>conte\u00fado` para cada linha ADICIONADA no \u00edndice.\n# O n\u00famero da linha \u00e9 o do arquivo NOVO, derivado do cabe\u00e7alho de hunk \u2014 assim o\n# relat\u00f3rio aponta para onde a pessoa precisa ir, n\u00e3o para um offset de diff.\nstream_staged() {\n  git -C \"$ROOT\" diff --cached --no-color -U0 --diff-filter=ACMRT 2>/dev/null | awk '\n    /^\\+\\+\\+ / {\n      f = substr($0, 5)\n      if (f == \"/dev/null\") { file = \"\" }\n      else { sub(/^[ab]\\//, \"\", f); do\n    [ -n \"$rel\" ] || continue\n    case \"$rel\" in .git/*|*/.git/*) continue ;; esac\n    if [ \"${rel:0:1}\" = '/' ]; then abs=\"$rel\"; else abs=\"$ROOT/$rel\"; fi\n    [ -f \"$abs\" ] || continue\n    sz=\"$(wc -c < \"$abs\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n    sz=\"${sz//[[:space:]]/}\"\n    if [ \"${sz:-0}\" -gt \"$MAX_BYTES\" ]; then\n      [ \"$VERBOSE\" -eq 1 ] && printf '  [pulado] %s (%s bytes > %s)\\n' \"$rel\" \"$sz\" \"$MAX_BYTES\" >&2\n      continue\n    fi\n    # -I descarta bin\u00e1rio; sem isso um .png com bytes felizes vira ru\u00eddo eterno.\n    while IFS= read -r hit; do\n      [ -n \"$hit\" ] || continue\n      lno=\"${hit%%:*}\"\n      content=\"${hit#*:}\"\n      printf '%s\\t%s\\t%s\\n' \"$rel\" \"$lno\" \"$content\"\n    done < <(grep -nIE -e \"$COMBINED\" -- \"$abs\" 2>/dev/null || true)\n  done\n}\n\n# \u2500\u2500 an\u00e1lise \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nFINDINGS=0\nSUPPRESSED=0\nMARKER_ON_REAL=0\nLINES_SEEN=0\n\nanalyze_stream() {\n  local stream=\"$1\" cand rec path lno content has_marker i tok key seen=''\n  cand=\"$(mktmp)\"\n  grep -E -e \"$COMBINED\" -- \"$stream\" > \"$cand\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\n  while IFS= read -r rec; do\n    [ -n \"$rec\" ] || continue\n    path=\"$(printf '%s' \"$rec\" | cut -f1)\"\n    lno=\"$(printf '%s' \"$rec\" | cut -f2)\"\n    content=\"$(printf '%s' \"$rec\" | cut -f3-)\"\n    LINES_SEEN=$((LINES_SEEN + 1))\n\n    has_marker=0\n    if [ -n \"$ALLOW_MARKER\" ] && printf '%s' \"$content\" | grep -qF -e \"$ALLOW_MARKER\"; then\n      has_marker=1\n    fi\n\n    for i in \"${!FAM_RE[@]}\"; do\n      while IFS= read -r tok; do\n        [ -n \"$tok\" ] || continue\n        key=\"$path|$lno|$tok\"\n        case \"$seen\" in *\"[$key]\"*) continue ;; esac\n        seen=\"$seen[$key]\"\n\n        if is_placeholder \"$tok\"; then\n          SUPPRESSED=$((SUPPRESSED + 1))\n          if [ \"$VERBOSE\" -eq 1 ]; then\n            if [ \"$has_marker\" -eq 1 ]; then\n              printf '  [placeholder+marcador] %s:%s  %s  %s\\n' \\\n                \"$path\" \"$lno\" \"${FAM_NAME[$i]}\" \"$(redact \"$tok\")\"\n            else\n              printf '  [placeholder] %s:%s  %s  %s\\n' \\\n                \"$path\" \"$lno\" \"${FAM_NAME[$i]}\" \"$(redact \"$tok\")\"\n            fi\n          fi\n          continue\n        fi\n\n        FINDINGS=$((FINDINGS + 1))\n        printf '  [CREDENCIAL] %s:%s\\n' \"$path\" \"$lno\"\n        printf '               fam\u00edlia : %s\\n' \"${FAM_NAME[$i]}\"\n        printf '               token   : %s   (redigido \u2014 um scanner que imprime o segredo \u00e9 o pr\u00f3ximo vazamento)\\n' \\\n          \"$(redact \"$tok\")\"\n        if [ \"$has_marker\" -eq 1 ]; then\n          MARKER_ON_REAL=$((MARKER_ON_REAL + 1))\n          printf '               ATEN\u00c7\u00c3O : a linha carrega `%s`, mas o token N\u00c3O \u00e9 placeholder.\\n' \"$ALLOW_MARKER\"\n          printf '                         O marcador n\u00e3o desarma um segredo real \u2014 s\u00f3 a rota\u00e7\u00e3o da\\n'\n          printf '                         credencial desarma. Ocorr\u00eancia mantida como falha.\\n'\n        fi\n      done < <(printf '%s\\n' \"$content\" | grep -oE -e \"${FAM_RE[$i]}\" 2>/dev/null || true)\n    done\n  done < \"$cand\"\n  return 0\n}\n\n# \u2500\u2500 motores externos \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nENGINE_RC=0\nrun_engines() {\n  local mode=\"$1\" eng adapter n=0 builtin_declared=0\n  while IFS= read -r eng; do\n    [ -n \"$eng\" ] || continue\n    n=$((n + 1))\n    if [ \"$eng\" = \"builtin\" ]; then builtin_declared=1; continue; fi\n    adapter=\"$ROOT/.harness/bin/secret-engine-${eng}.sh\"\n    if [ ! -x \"$adapter\" ] && [ ! -r \"$adapter\" ]; then\n      printf 'secret-scan: motor `%s` declarado em secrets.engines, mas o adaptador\\n' \"$eng\" >&2\n      printf '             .harness/bin/secret-engine-%s.sh n\u00e3o existe \u2014 motor N\u00c3O EXECUTADO.\\n' \"$eng\" >&2\n      printf '             Voc\u00ea acredita rodar com %s e est\u00e1 rodando s\u00f3 com builtin.\\n' \"$eng\" >&2\n      continue\n    fi\n    if ! have \"$eng\"; then\n      printf 'secret-scan: adaptador de `%s` existe mas o bin\u00e1rio n\u00e3o est\u00e1 no PATH \u2014 N\u00c3O EXECUTADO.\\n' \"$eng\" >&2\n      continue\n    fi\n    printf '  \u2192 motor externo: %s (%s %s)\\n' \"$eng\" \"${adapter#\"$ROOT\"/}\" \"$mode\"\n    if ! bash \"$adapter\" \"$mode\"; then\n      ENGINE_RC=1\n      printf '  [CREDENCIAL] motor externo `%s` reportou achado (veja a sa\u00edda acima).\\n' \"$eng\"\n    fi\n  done < <(cfg_list secrets.engines)\n\n  if [ \"$n\" -eq 0 ]; then\n    printf 'secret-scan: `secrets.engines` vazio \u2014 rodando apenas o builtin.\\n' >&2\n  elif [ \"$builtin_declared\" -eq 0 ]; then\n    printf 'secret-scan: `builtin` n\u00e3o est\u00e1 em secrets.engines, mas foi executado mesmo assim.\\n' >&2\n    printf '             Este scanner \u00e9 o piso do harness; deslig\u00e1-lo exige secrets.scan_enabled=false.\\n' >&2\n  fi\n  return 0\n}\n\n# \u2500\u2500 modos \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\ncmd_patterns() {\n  local i\n  printf 'fam\u00edlias de credencial (ERE POSIX, usadas com `grep -oE`)\\n\\n'\n  for i in \"${!FAM_NAME[@]}\"; do\n    printf '  %-30s %s\\n' \"${FAM_NAME[$i]}\" \"${FAM_RE[$i]}\"\n  done\n  printf '\\nallowlist (aplicada ao TOKEN, jamais \u00e0 linha): example \u00b7 your_ \u00b7 your- \u00b7 xxxx \u00b7 changeme \u00b7 000000 \u00b7 <\u2026>\\n'\n  printf 'fora da allowlist por decis\u00e3o expl\u00edcita: test \u00b7 mock \u00b7 fake \u00b7 sample \u00b7 dummy \u00b7 fingerprint \u00b7 digest \u00b7 sha256\\n'\n  printf 'marcador de supress\u00e3o: %s (vale s\u00f3 na mesma linha E s\u00f3 sobre token placeholder)\\n' \"$ALLOW_MARKER\"\n  return 0\n}\n\nreport_and_exit() {\n  local mode=\"$1\"\n  printf '\\n'\n  if [ \"$FINDINGS\" -eq 0 ] && [ \"$ENGINE_RC\" -eq 0 ]; then\n    printf 'secret-scan: 0 credenciais em %s linha(s) candidata(s) [modo %s].\\n' \"$LINES_SEEN\" \"$mode\"\n    if [ \"$SUPPRESSED\" -gt 0 ]; then\n      printf '             %s ocorr\u00eancia(s) tratada(s) como placeholder (use --verbose para v\u00ea-las).\\n' \"$SUPPRESSED\"\n    fi\n    exit 0\n  fi\n  printf 'secret-scan: %s CREDENCIAL(IS) encontrada(s) [modo %s].\\n' \"$FINDINGS\" \"$mode\" >&2\n  if [ \"$MARKER_ON_REAL\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    printf '             %s delas carregam o marcador `%s` indevidamente.\\n' \"$MARKER_ON_REAL\" \"$ALLOW_MARKER\" >&2\n  fi\n  {\n    printf '\\n'\n    printf 'A\u00e7\u00e3o necess\u00e1ria, nesta ordem:\\n'\n    printf '  1. ROTACIONE a credencial no provedor. Ela j\u00e1 vazou para o disco; remover\\n'\n    printf '     a linha n\u00e3o a revoga.\\n'\n    printf '  2. Tire o valor do c\u00f3digo e leia-o do ambiente ou do cofre do projeto.\\n'\n    printf '  3. S\u00f3 ent\u00e3o recomite.\\n'\n    printf '\\n'\n    printf 'Se o valor \u00e9 comprovadamente um placeholder, escreva-o como placeholder\\n'\n    printf '(EXAMPLE / YOUR_\u2026 / xxxx / <\u2026>) \u2014 n\u00e3o tente silenciar o scanner com coment\u00e1rio.\\n'\n    printf '\\n'\n  } >&2\n  exit 1\n}\n\ncmd_scan() {\n  local mode=\"$1\"; shift\n  local stream\n\n  if [ \"$SCAN_ENABLED\" = \"false\" ]; then\n    printf 'secret-scan: DESLIGADO (secrets.scan_enabled=false) \u2014 nenhuma varredura executada.\\n' >&2\n    printf '             Este verde \u00e9 desligamento, n\u00e3o aprova\u00e7\u00e3o.\\n' >&2\n    exit 0\n  fi\n\n  stream=\"$(mktmp)\"\n  case \"$mode\" in\n    staged)\n      if ! in_git_repo; then\n        printf 'secret-scan: o modo staged exige um reposit\u00f3rio git (raiz testada: %s).\\n' \"$ROOT\" >&2\n        printf '             Use `--all` ou passe caminhos expl\u00edcitos.\\n' >&2\n        exit 3\n      fi\n      stream_staged > \"$stream\"\n      ;;\n    all)\n      list_all_files | stream_files > \"$stream\"\n      ;;\n    files)\n      printf '%s\\n' \"$@\" | stream_files > \"$stream\"\n      ;;\n  esac\n\n  analyze_stream \"$stream\"\n  run_engines \"$mode\"\n  report_and_exit \"$mode\"\n}\n\n# \u2500\u2500 selftest \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n# Um scanner nunca visto vermelho n\u00e3o \u00e9 um scanner. E um scanner que s\u00f3 foi visto\n# vermelho no caso f\u00e1cil (segredo nu) n\u00e3o prova nada sobre o defeito D1 \u2014 por isso\n# o PROBE_B repete o MESMO segredo com o coment\u00e1rio que absolvia no harness de origem.\nSELFTEST_DIR=''\nselftest_cleanup() {\n  [ -n \"${SELFTEST_DIR:-}\" ] && [ -d \"${SELFTEST_DIR:-}\" ] && rm -rf \"$SELFTEST_DIR\"\n  cleanup_tmp\n  return 0\n}\n\nprobe_run_case() {\n  local dir=\"$1\" desc=\"$2\" expected=\"$3\"\n  local rc=0 out\n  out=\"$dir/.out\"\n  ( cd \"$dir\" && bash \"$SELF\" --all ) > \"$out\" 2>&1 || rc=$?\n  if [ \"$rc\" -eq \"$expected\" ]; then\n    printf '  [PASS] exit %s (esperado %s) \u2014 %s\\n' \"$rc\" \"$expected\" \"$desc\"\n    return 0\n  fi\n  printf '  [FAIL] exit %s (esperado %s) \u2014 %s\\n' \"$rc\" \"$expected\" \"$desc\"\n  sed -e 's/^/         | /' \"$out\"\n  return 1\n}\n\ncmd_selftest() {\n  local fails=0 d\n  SELFTEST_DIR=\"$(mktemp -d \"${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/harness-secret-selftest.XXXXXX\")\"\n  trap selftest_cleanup EXIT\n\n  # Os segredos sint\u00e9ticos s\u00e3o montados por CONCATENA\u00c7\u00c3O: se aparecessem literais\n  # neste arquivo, o pr\u00f3prio scanner (probe G) acusaria o scanner. Um selftest que\n  # contamina a \u00e1rvore que ele testa n\u00e3o \u00e9 um selftest.\n  local P1='AKIA' P2='Z7Q4RT2VBN6XKD9L'\n  local REAL=\"${P1}${P2}\"\n  local FAKE=\"${P1}IOSFODNN7EXAMPLE\"\n\n  printf 'secret-scan --selftest \u00b7 \u00e1rvore sint\u00e9tica em %s\\n' \"$SELFTEST_DIR\"\n  printf 'marcador de supress\u00e3o em vigor: %s\\n\\n' \"$ALLOW_MARKER\"\n\n  printf 'Probes obrigat\u00f3rios (a regress\u00e3o do defeito D1 \u00e9 o PROBE_B)\\n'\n\n  d=\"$SELFTEST_DIR/a\"; mkdir -p \"$d\"\n  printf 'aws_key = %s\\n' \"$REAL\" > \"$d/config.txt\"\n  probe_run_case \"$d\" 'PROBE_A \u2014 segredo nu deve ser detectado' 1 || fails=$((fails + 1))\n\n  d=\"$SELFTEST_DIR/b\"; mkdir -p \"$d\"\n  printf 'aws_key = %s  // test-only\\n' \"$REAL\" > \"$d/config.txt\"\n  probe_run_case \"$d\" 'PROBE_B \u2014 MESMO segredo + coment\u00e1rio `test-only` na linha (D1)' 1 || fails=$((fails + 1))\n\n  d=\"$SELFTEST_DIR/c\"; mkdir -p \"$d\"\n  printf 'aws_key = %s\\n' \"$FAKE\" > \"$d/config.txt\"\n  probe_run_case \"$d\" 'PROBE_C \u2014 placeholder \u00f3bvio n\u00e3o \u00e9 achado' 0 || fails=$((fails + 1))\n\n  printf '\\nProbes complementares (sem\u00e2ntica do marcador e auto-imunidade)\\n'\n\n  d=\"$SELFTEST_DIR/dd\"; mkdir -p \"$d\"\n  printf 'aws_key = %s  # %s\\n' \"$REAL\" \"$ALLOW_MARKER\" > \"$d/config.txt\"\n  probe_run_case \"$d\" 'PROBE_D \u2014 marcador sobre token REAL n\u00e3o desarma' 1 || fails=$((fails + 1))\n\n  d=\"$SELFTEST_DIR/e\"; mkdir -p \"$d\"\n  printf 'aws_key = %s  # %s\\n' \"$FAKE\" \"$ALLOW_MARKER\" > \"$d/config.txt\"\n  probe_run_case \"$d\" 'PROBE_E \u2014 marcador sobre placeholder \u00e9 aceito' 0 || fails=$((fails + 1))\n\n  d=\"$SELFTEST_DIR/f\"; mkdir -p \"$d\"\n  printf 'const total = soma(a, b);\\n' > \"$d/app.js\"\n  probe_run_case \"$d\" 'PROBE_F \u2014 c\u00f3digo limpo sai 0' 0 || fails=$((fails + 1))\n\n  d=\"$SELFTEST_DIR/g\"; mkdir -p \"$d/.harness/bin\" \"$d/.harness/lib\"\n  cp -- \"$SELF\" \"$d/.harness/bin/secret-scan.sh\"\n  cp -- \"$LIB\" \"$d/.harness/lib/harness-lib.sh\"\n  probe_run_case \"$d\" 'PROBE_G \u2014 o scanner n\u00e3o acusa o pr\u00f3prio c\u00f3digo-fonte' 0 || fails=$((fails + 1))\n\n  d=\"$SELFTEST_DIR/h\"; mkdir -p \"$d\"\n  {\n    printf 'DATABASE_URL=postgres://app:%s@db.interno:5432/prod\\n' 'S3nh4Sup3rS3cr3t4'\n    printf 'jwt = %s.%s.%s\\n' 'eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9' 'eyJzdWIiOiIxMjM0NTY3ODkwIn0' 'dBjftJeZ4CVPmB92K27uhbUJU1p1r'\n  } > \"$d/env.txt\"\n  probe_run_case \"$d\" 'PROBE_H \u2014 string de conex\u00e3o com senha e JWT' 1 || fails=$((fails + 1))\n\n  printf '\\n'\n  if [ \"$fails\" -eq 0 ]; then\n    printf 'selftest: TODOS OS PROBES PASSARAM \u2014 o scanner foi visto vermelho e verde,\\n'\n    printf '          e o caso que derrubou o scanner de origem (D1) est\u00e1 coberto.\\n'\n    return 0\n  fi\n  printf 'selftest: %s PROBE(S) FALHARAM \u2014 o PR\u00d3PRIO scanner est\u00e1 quebrado neste ambiente.\\n' \"$fails\" >&2\n  printf '          N\u00e3o confie em nenhum verde dele at\u00e9 que isto seja corrigido.\\n' >&2\n  return 9\n}\n\n# \u2500\u2500 despacho \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nMODE=staged\nVERBOSE=0\nFILES=()\n\nwhile [ $# -gt 0 ]; do\n  case \"$1\" in\n    --all)      MODE=all ;;\n    --staged)   MODE=staged ;;\n    --verbose|-v) VERBOSE=1 ;;\n    --patterns) MODE=patterns ;;\n    --selftest) MODE=selftest ;;\n    -h|--help)  usage; exit 0 ;;\n    --*)\n      printf 'secret-scan: argumento desconhecido: %s\\n\\n' \"$1\" >&2\n      usage >&2\n      exit 3 ;;\n    *)          FILES+=(\"$1\"); MODE=files ;;\n  esac\n  shift\ndone\n\ncase \"$MODE\" in\n  patterns) cmd_patterns; exit 0 ;;\n  selftest) cmd_selftest; exit $? ;;\n  files)    cmd_scan files \"${FILES[@]}\" ;;\n  *)        cmd_scan \"$MODE\" ;;\nesac\n"
}

> (EXAMPLE / YOUR_… / xxxx / <…>) — não tente silenciar o scanner com comentário.

> EXIT=1
> --- patterns ---
> famílias de credencial (ERE POSIX, usadas com `grep -oE`)
> 
>   chave-de-api (sk/pk/rk)        (sk|pk|rk)_(live|test)_[A-Za-z0-9]{10,}|sk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}
>   aws-access-key-id              (AKIA|ASIA|AROA|AIDA|AGPA|ANPA)[0-9A-Z]{16}
>   google-api-key                 AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}
>   json-web-token                 eyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_=-]{8,}
>   token-github                   gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{20,}
>   chave-privada-pem              -----BEGIN [A-Z ]*PRIVATE KEY-----
>   string-de-conexao-com-senha    [a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9+.-]*://[^:@/[:space:]]+:[^@/[:space:]]{6,}@[^[:space:]"'`]+
>   atribuicao-de-credencial       (api[_-]?key|apikey|secret|token|password|passwd|access[_-]?key|private[_-]?key|client[_-]?secret|auth[_-]?token)["']?[[:space:]]*[:=][[:space:]]*["'][^"'[:space:]]{16,}["']
> 
> allowlist (aplicada ao TOKEN, jamais à linha): example · your_ · your- · xxxx · changeme · 000000 · <…>
> fora da allowlist por decisão explícita: test · mock · fake · sample · dummy · fingerprint · digest · sha256
> marcador de supressão: harness-scan:allow (vale só na mesma linha E só sobre token placeholder)

CLAUDE.md\\n'\nwrite_claude_md\n\n# \u2500\u2500 3. diret\u00f3rios de paths.* \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\ndir_readme() {\n  case \"$1\" in\n    specs)        printf 'Su\u00edtes de especifica\u00e7\u00e3o, uma pasta por m\u00f3dulo. O Spec Gate l\u00ea o marcador de estado no \u00edndice de cada su\u00edte.\\n' ;;\n    processes)    printf 'Processos humanos documentados (Lei 2): Operador \u00b7 Sequ\u00eancia \u00b7 Gates de verifica\u00e7\u00e3o \u00b7 Recupera\u00e7\u00e3o \u00b7 Sinal de sucesso.\\n' ;;\n    architecture) printf 'Decis\u00f5es de arquitetura e seus trade-offs, uma por arquivo, datadas.\\n' ;;\n    archive)      printf 'Selos de sess\u00e3o arquivados, preservados para trilha hist\u00f3rica.\\n' ;;\n    knowledge)    printf 'Registro de observa\u00e7\u00f5es: o que foi constru\u00eddo, qual requisito atende, e o que se aprendeu que n\u00e3o estava na especifica\u00e7\u00e3o.\\n' ;;\n    *)            printf 'Diret\u00f3rio declarado em `paths.%s` do contrato do harness.\\n' \"$1\" ;;\n  esac\n}\n\nprintf '\\n3. Estrutura de paths.*\\n'\nwhile IFS= read -r pk; do\n  [ -n \"$pk\" ] || continue\n  case \"$pk\" in _*) continue ;; esac\n  pval=\"$(cfg \"paths.$pk\")\"\n  [ -n \"$pval\" ] || continue\n  case \"$pval\" in /*) line_info \"paths.$pk \u00e9 absoluto ($pval) \u2014 ignorado; o kit n\u00e3o cria caminho fora do reposit\u00f3rio.\"; continue ;; esac\n\n  base=\"$(basename -- \"$pval\")\"\n  case \"$base\" in\n    *.*)\n      # \u00c9 um arquivo declarado (ex.: o selo de sess\u00e3o). Garante o diret\u00f3rio-pai e\n      # N\u00c3O cria o arquivo: um arquivo vazio parece um artefato preenchido.\n      parent=\"$(dirname -- \"$pval\")\"\n      if [ \"$parent\" != \".\" ] && [ ! -d \"$TARGET/$parent\" ]; then\n        ensure_dir \"$TARGET/$parent\" && line_dir \"$parent/\"\n      fi\n      line_info \"paths.$pk = $pval (arquivo \u2014 criado no primeiro uso, n\u00e3o pelo instalador)\"\n      continue\n      ;;\n  esac\n\n  if [ ! -d \"$TARGET/$pval\" ]; then\n    ensure_dir \"$TARGET/$pval\" && line_dir \"$pval/\"\n  fi\n  if [ ! -e \"$TARGET/$pval/.gitkeep\" ]; then\n    if [ \"$DRY\" -eq 0 ]; then : > \"$TARGET/$pval/.gitkeep\"; fi\n    line_new \"$pval/.gitkeep\"\n  fi\n  if [ ! -e \"$TARGET/$pval/README.md\" ]; then\n    if [ \"$DRY\" -eq 0 ]; then dir_readme \"$pk\" > \"$TARGET/$pval/README.md\"; fi\n    line_new \"$pval/README.md\"\n  else\n    line_same \"$pval/README.md\"\n  fi\ndone < <(cfg_keys paths)\n\n# \u2500\u2500 4. .gitignore \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nprintf '\\n4. .gitignore\\n'\nGITIGNORE=\"$TARGET/.gitignore\"\nGI_WANT='.claude/settings.local.json\n.harness/proof/'\n\ngi_missing=''\nwhile IFS= read -r want; then\n  if [ \"$DRY\" -eq 0 ]; then\n    {\n      if [ -s \"$GITIGNORE\" ] && [ -n \"$(tail -c 1 \"$GITIGNORE\" 2>/dev/null)\" ]; then printf '\\n'; fi\n      printf '# --- Harness Kit v%s ---\\n' \"$KIT_VERSION\"\n      printf '# Configura\u00e7\u00e3o local da m\u00e1quina e artefatos de sess\u00e3o: nunca versionados.\\n'\n      printf '%s' \"$gi_missing\"\n    } >> \"$GITIGNORE\"\n  fi\n  while IFS= read -r m; do\n    [ -n \"$m\" ] || continue\n    line_new \".gitignore: $m\"\n  done <<< \"$gi_missing\"\nfi\n\n# \u2500\u2500 5. pr\u00f3ximos passos + doctor \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nnext_steps() {\n  cat <<'STEPS'\n\n\u2550\u2550 Pr\u00f3ximos passos \u2014 Fase B do ritual (a fase que n\u00e3o pode ser pulada) \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n\nO harness est\u00e1 no disco. Ele ainda N\u00c3O sabe nada sobre este reposit\u00f3rio: os probes\nest\u00e3o nulos, os pap\u00e9is vazios e os gatilhos do Spec Gate em branco. Nesse estado o\ndoctor falha de prop\u00f3sito, e o Spec Gate nunca dispara.\n\nResponda (ou derive do reposit\u00f3rio e apresente para confirma\u00e7\u00e3o) e grave em\n`.harness/harness.config.json`:\n\n  project.name / project.slug     Como este projeto se chama.\n  roles.reviewer                  Quem aprova antes de merge/deploy.\n  roles.owner                     Quem absorve o preju\u00edzo se quebrar em produ\u00e7\u00e3o.\n  probes.typecheck.cmd            Comando que prova que os tipos/compila\u00e7\u00e3o est\u00e3o s\u00e3os.\n  probes.test.cmd                 Comando que roda os testes.\n  probes.lint.cmd                 Comando que faz lint.\n  probes.build.cmd                Comando que builda.\n  probes.install.cmd              Comando que instala as depend\u00eancias.\n  spec_gate.trigger_paths         Globs que significam \"c\u00f3digo de m\u00f3dulo novo\" AQUI.\n  law2_process.sop_map            Quando exigir processo escrito antes do c\u00f3digo.\n\nREGRA DE OURO DOS PROBES: rode cada comando ANTES de grav\u00e1-lo, e registre o exit\ncode observado em `expect_exit`. Um probe n\u00e3o executado n\u00e3o \u00e9 um probe. Se a\ncapacidade n\u00e3o existe neste stack, grave `null` \u2014 string vazia significa \"n\u00e3o\nentrevistado\" e o doctor falha nela de prop\u00f3sito.\n\nDepois:\n\n  bash .harness/bin/doctor.sh --deep      # Gate C: precisa sair 0\n  bash .harness/bin/spec-gate.sh --explain # Gate D: confirme que um gatilho casa\n  bash .harness/bin/install-hooks.sh      # opcional: gates no pre-commit\n  bash .harness/bin/secret-scan.sh --selftest\n\nE a Fase E (prova de ingest\u00e3o), em sess\u00e3o limpa: pe\u00e7a ao modelo para imprimir\nverbatim o can\u00e1rio do harness. Se ele n\u00e3o devolver a string, a doutrina n\u00e3o est\u00e1\nsendo carregada e nenhum gate deste harness \u00e9 confi\u00e1vel.\n\nSTEPS\n}\n\nprintf '\\n5. Resumo\\n'\nprintf '      %s criado(s) \u00b7 %s id\u00eantico(s) \u00b7 %s conflito(s) \u00b7 %s diret\u00f3rio(s) \u00b7 %s ausente(s) no kit\\n' \\\n  \"$N_NEW\" \"$N_SAME\" \"$N_CONFLICT\" \"$N_DIR\" \"$N_SKIP\"\n\nif [ \"$N_CONFLICT\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  printf '\\n      CONFLITOS \u2014 o instalador N\u00c3O sobrescreveu nada. Revise e mescle \u00e0 m\u00e3o:\\n'\n  while IFS= read -r c; do\n    [ -n \"$c\" ] || continue\n    printf '        %s   (vers\u00e3o do kit em %s.harness-new)\\n' \"$c\" \"$c\"\n  done <<< \"$CONFLICT_LIST\"\n  printf '\\n      Em reinstala\u00e7\u00e3o, `.harness/harness.config.json` aparecer aqui \u00e9 ESPERADO:\\n'\n  printf '      o do destino carrega a entrevista da Fase B e n\u00e3o deve ser trocado pelo pristino.\\n'\nfi\n\nnext_steps\n\nRC=0\n[ \"$N_CONFLICT\" -gt 0 ] && RC=1\n\nif [ \"$DRY\" -eq 1 ]; then\n  printf '(dry-run: nenhum byte foi escrito; o doctor n\u00e3o foi executado.)\\n'\n  exit \"$RC\"\nfi\n\nif [ \"$RUN_DOCTOR\" -eq 1 ] && [ -r \"$TARGET/.harness/bin/doctor.sh\" ]; then\n  printf '\\n\u2550\u2550 Diagn\u00f3stico (doctor.sh) \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\\n'\n  drc=0\n  ( cd \"$TARGET\" && HARNESS_CONFIG=\"$TARGET/.harness/harness.config.json\" bash .harness/bin/doctor.sh ) || drc=$?\n  printf '\\n      doctor.sh saiu %s.\\n' \"$drc\"\n  if [ \"$drc\" -ne 0 ]; then\n    printf '      Isto \u00e9 ESPERADO antes da Fase B: o contrato ainda est\u00e1 em branco.\\n'\n    printf '      O exit code do doctor N\u00c3O \u00e9 o exit code da instala\u00e7\u00e3o.\\n'\n  fi\nfi\n\nexit \"$RC\"\n"

Se j\u00e1 existir um\n#   hook l\u00e1, ele para de rodar silenciosamente no instante em que o caminho muda.\n#   Este script detecta esse caso e AVISA em vez de deixar a pessoa descobrir depois\n#   que a verifica\u00e7\u00e3o dela sumiu. then\n      info \"$HOOKS_REL/pre-commit j\u00e1 est\u00e1 atualizado.\"\n      rm -f \"$tmp\"\n    else\n      if [ \"$DRY\" -eq 0 ]; then\n        cat \"$tmp\" > \"$hook\"\n        chmod +x \"$hook\"\n      fi\n      rm -f \"$tmp\"\n      info \"$HOOKS_REL/pre-commit escrito (+x).\"\n    fi\n  fi\n\n  # 2. hooks legados que deixar\u00e3o de rodar\n  if [ -d \"$GITDIR/hooks\" ]; then\n    local legacy found=0\n    for legacy in \"$GITDIR/hooks\"/*; do\n      [ -f \"$legacy\" ] || continue\n      case \"$legacy\" in *.sample) continue ;; esac\n      if [ \"$found\" -eq 0 ]; then\n        printf '\\n      AVISO: existem hooks em .git/hooks/ que PARAM de rodar quando\\n' >&2\n        printf '             core.hooksPath muda (o git substitui o diret\u00f3rio, n\u00e3o soma):\\n' >&2\n        found=1\n      fi\n      printf '               %s\\n' \"$(basename -- \"$legacy\")\" >&2\n    done\n    if [ \"$found\" -eq 1 ]; then\n      printf '             Para preserv\u00e1-los, chame-os a partir de %s/pre-commit.\\n\\n' \"$HOOKS_REL\" >&2\n    fi\n  fi\n\n  # 3. core.hooksPath (com backup do valor anterior)\n  if [ \"$CURRENT\" = \"$HOOKS_REL\" ]; then\n    info 'core.hooksPath j\u00e1 aponta para o harness \u2014 nada a mudar.'\n  else\n    if [ \"$DRY\" -eq 0 ]; then\n      if [ -n \"$CURRENT\" ]; then printf '%s\\n' \"$CURRENT\" > \"$BACKUP\"; else printf '%s\\n' \"$UNSET_SENTINEL\" > \"$BACKUP\"; fi\n      git config core.hooksPath \"$HOOKS_REL\"\n    fi\n    info \"core.hooksPath = $HOOKS_REL  (valor anterior guardado em .git/$BACKUP_NAME)\"\n  fi\n\n  printf '\\n      Gates encadeados no pre-commit, nesta ordem:\\n'\n  printf '        1. secret-scan.sh   (ligado por secrets.scan_enabled)\\n'\n  printf '        2. spec-gate.sh     (ligado por spec_gate.enabled)\\n'\n  printf '        3. sop-gate.sh      (ligado por law2_process.enabled)\\n'\n  printf '\\n      Desligar um deles pontualmente: HARNESS_HOOK_SPEC_GATE=0 git commit \u2026\\n'\n  printf '      Desligar todos:                  HARNESS_SKIP_HOOKS=1 git commit \u2026\\n'\n  printf '      Verifique agora, antes de confiar:  bash %s/pre-commit\\n\\n' \"$HOOKS_REL\"\n  return \"$rc\"\n}\n\n# \u2500\u2500 selftest \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nSELFTEST_ROOT=''\nselftest_cleanup() {\n  [ -n \"${SELFTEST_ROOT:-}\" ] && [ -d \"${SELFTEST_ROOT:-}\" ] && rm -rf \"$SELFTEST_ROOT\"\n  return 0\n}\n\ncmd_selftest() {\n  local fails=0 t rc got\n  if ! command -v git >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    printf 'install-hooks --selftest: git indispon\u00edvel \u2014 nada a exercitar.\\n' >&2\n    return 9\n  fi\n  SELFTEST_ROOT=\"$(mktemp -d \"${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/harness-hooks-selftest.XXXXXX\")\"\n  trap selftest_cleanup EXIT\n\n  _a() {\n    local desc=\"$1\"; shift\n    if \"$@\"; then printf '  [PASS] %s\\n' \"$desc\"; return 0; fi\n    printf '  [FAIL] %s\\n' \"$desc\"; fails=$((fails + 1)); return 1\n  }\n\n  t=\"$SELFTEST_ROOT/repo\"\n  mkdir -p \"$t/.harness\"\n  cp -R \"$HARNESS_DIR_LOCAL/bin\" \"$t/.harness/bin\"\n  cp -R \"$HARNESS_DIR_LOCAL/lib\" \"$t/.harness/lib\"\n  cp -- \"$HARNESS_DIR_LOCAL/harness.config.json\" \"$t/.harness/harness.config.json\"\n  chmod +x \"$t\"/.harness/bin/*.sh 2>/dev/null || true\n  ( cd \"$t\" && git init -q . ) >/dev/null 2>&1\n  printf '# baseline\\n' > \"$t/README.md\"\n  ( cd \"$t\" && git add -A ) >/dev/null 2>&1\n\n  printf 'install-hooks --selftest \u00b7 reposit\u00f3rio sint\u00e9tico em %s\\n\\n' \"$t\"\n\n  printf 'A. instala\u00e7\u00e3o em reposit\u00f3rio limpo\\n'\n  rc=0; ( cd \"$t\" && bash .harness/bin/install-hooks.sh ) >/dev/null 2>&1 || rc=$?\n  _a 'exit 0'                       test \"$rc\" -eq 0\n  got=\"$( cd \"$t\" && git config --get core.hooksPath 2>/dev/null || true )\"\n  _a 'core.hooksPath aponta ao kit' test \"$got\" = \"$HOOKS_REL\"\n  _a 'pre-commit existe e \u00e9 execut\u00e1vel' test -x \"$t/$HOOKS_REL/pre-commit\"\n  _a 'pre-commit carrega a assinatura'  grep -qF -e \"$SIGNATURE\" \"$t/$HOOKS_REL/pre-commit\"\n\n  printf '\\nB. reinstala\u00e7\u00e3o \u00e9 idempotente\\n'\n  rc=0; ( cd \"$t\" && bash .harness/bin/install-hooks.sh ) >/dev/null 2>&1 || rc=$?\n  _a 'exit 0' test \"$rc\" -eq 0\n\n  printf '\\nC. o hook realmente bloqueia (Lei 1: um gate nunca visto vermelho n\u00e3o \u00e9 um gate)\\n'\n  rc=0; ( cd \"$t\" && bash \"$HOOKS_REL/pre-commit\" ) >/dev/null 2>&1 || rc=$?\n  _a '\u00e1rvore limpa \u2192 hook passa (exit 0)' test \"$rc\" -eq 0\n  # Segredo montado por concatena\u00e7\u00e3o para n\u00e3o contaminar o pr\u00f3prio arquivo.\n  local S1='AKIA' S2='Z7Q4RT2VBN6XKD9L'\n  printf 'aws = %s%s\\n' \"$S1\" \"$S2\" > \"$t/vazamento.env\"\n  ( cd \"$t\" && git add vazamento.env ) >/dev/null 2>&1\n  rc=0; ( cd \"$t\" && bash \"$HOOKS_REL/pre-commit\" ) >/dev/null 2>&1 || rc=$?\n  _a 'credencial no \u00edndice \u2192 hook bloqueia (exit != 0)' test \"$rc\" -ne 0\n  rc=0; ( cd \"$t\" && HARNESS_SKIP_HOOKS=1 bash \"$HOOKS_REL/pre-commit\" ) >/dev/null 2>&1 || rc=$?\n  _a 'HARNESS_SKIP_HOOKS=1 pula tudo (exit 0)' test \"$rc\" -eq 0\n  ( cd \"$t\" && git rm -q --cached vazamento.env ) >/dev/null 2>&1\n  rm -f \"$t/vazamento.env\"\n\n  printf '\\nD. desinstala\u00e7\u00e3o restaura o estado anterior\\n'\n  rc=0; ( cd \"$t\" && bash .harness/bin/install-hooks.sh --uninstall ) >/dev/null 2>&1 || rc=$?\n  _a 'exit 0' test \"$rc\" -eq 0\n  got=\"$( cd \"$t\" && git config --get core.hooksPath 2>/dev/null || true )\"\n  _a 'core.hooksPath voltou a n\u00e3o existir' test -z \"$got\"\n  _a 'arquivos do hook preservados (desligar != destruir)' test -f \"$t/$HOOKS_REL/pre-commit\"\n\n  printf '\\nE. core.hooksPath de outro dono \u00e9 RECUSADO\\n'\n  ( cd \"$t\" && mkdir -p .githooks-do-time && git config core.hooksPath .githooks-do-time ) >/dev/null 2>&1\n  rc=0; ( cd \"$t\" && bash .harness/bin/install-hooks.sh ) >/dev/null 2>&1 || rc=$?\n  _a 'exit 1 (recusa)' test \"$rc\" -eq 1\n  got=\"$( cd \"$t\" && git config --get core.hooksPath 2>/dev/null || true )\"\n  _a 'caminho do outro dono INTACTO' test \"$got\" = '.githooks-do-time'\n\n  printf '\\nF. --force assume, e --uninstall devolve\\n'\n  rc=0; ( cd \"$t\" && bash .harness/bin/install-hooks.sh --force ) >/dev/null 2>&1 || rc=$?\n  _a 'exit 0' test \"$rc\" -eq 0\n  got=\"$( cd \"$t\" && git config --get core.hooksPath 2>/dev/null || true )\"\n  _a 'core.hooksPath assumido pelo kit' test \"$got\" = \"$HOOKS_REL\"\n  rc=0; ( cd \"$t\" && bash .harness/bin/install-hooks.sh --uninstall ) >/dev/null 2>&1 || rc=$?\n  _a 'exit 0' test \"$rc\" -eq 0\n  got=\"$( cd \"$t\" && git config --get core.hooksPath 2>/dev/null || true )\"\n  _a 'valor do outro dono RESTAURADO' test \"$got\" = '.githooks-do-time'\n\n  printf '\\n'\n  if [ \"$fails\" -eq 0 ]; then\n    printf 'selftest: TODOS OS CASOS PASSARAM \u2014 instala\u00e7\u00e3o, bloqueio, recusa e restaura\u00e7\u00e3o provados.\\n'\n    return 0\n  fi\n  printf 'selftest: %s CASO(S) FALHARAM \u2014 n\u00e3o confie neste instalador de hooks.\\n' \"$fails\" >&2\n  return 9\n}\n\n# \u2500\u2500 despacho \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nRC=0\ncase \"$ACTION\" in\n  selftest)  cmd_selftest || RC=$? ;;\n  uninstall) cmd_uninstall || RC=$? ;;\n  install)   cmd_install || RC=$? ;;\nesac\nexit \"$RC\"\n"
}

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n#\n# proof-audit.sh \u2014 auditoria MEC\u00c2NICA da Lei 1 (Materialidade).\n#\n# PURPOSE\n#   A Lei 1 exige prova f\u00edsica para toda afirma\u00e7\u00e3o de sucesso. Auto-avalia\u00e7\u00e3o n\u00e3o\n#   satisfaz a lei: quem afirmou \u00e9 a \u00faltima pessoa que deveria julgar a afirma\u00e7\u00e3o.\n#   Este script l\u00ea o manifesto de provas do selo e VERIFICA CADA CLAIM DE FORMA\n#   INDEPENDENTE \u2014 `git cat-file` para hash, `ls` para arquivo, re-execu\u00e7\u00e3o para\n#   comando, requisi\u00e7\u00e3o real para status HTTP, adaptador do projeto para registro.\n#\n#   O veredito \u00e9 o EXIT CODE: o n\u00famero de claims que n\u00e3o se sustentam.\n#\n# A REGRA CR\u00cdTICA (o defeito que este arquivo existe para n\u00e3o repetir)\n#   Claim cujo verificador N\u00c3O existe neste ambiente \u00e9 reportada `N\u00c3O VERIFIC\u00c1VEL`\n#   e CONTA COMO FALHA sempre que `seal.sinks` incluir `{kind:\"file\"}`.\n#   Motivo medido: na auditoria de origem, \"sem verificador \u2192 pula\" fez a auditoria\n#   degradar at\u00e9 checar apenas o que sempre existe \u2014 \"o commit existe e o arquivo\n#   existe\" \u2014 enquanto as claims caras (banco, servi\u00e7o, integra\u00e7\u00e3o) passavam\n#   despercebidas. `skip` que nunca bloqueia \u00e9 `pass` com outro nome.\n#\n#   Aus\u00eancia de prova tamb\u00e9m n\u00e3o \u00e9 prova: manifesto inexistente sai 1, n\u00e3o 0.\n#\n# USAGE\n#   proof-audit.sh [<manifesto|diret\u00f3rio>] [--all] [--no-exec] [--strict|--no-strict]\n#                  [--selftest] [--help]\n#     <caminho>     manifesto .json, ou diret\u00f3rio contendo manifestos\n#                   (padr\u00e3o: `seal.proof_manifest` do contrato)\n#     --all         audita TODOS os .json do diret\u00f3rio (padr\u00e3o: s\u00f3 o mais recente)\n#     --no-exec     n\u00e3o re-executa `command_exit`; rebaixa essas claims a N\u00c3O VERIFIC\u00c1VEL\n#     --strict      N\u00c3O VERIFIC\u00c1VEL conta como falha (padr\u00e3o quando h\u00e1 sink de arquivo)\n#     --no-strict   N\u00c3O VERIFIC\u00c1VEL apenas avisa \u2014 use com consci\u00eancia e diga por qu\u00ea\n#     --selftest    prova que o auditor detecta claim falsa e claim inverific\u00e1vel\n#\n# FORMATO DO MANIFESTO (JSON)\n#   Um array de claims, ou um objeto com a chave `claims`:\n#\n#   [\n#     { \"type\": \"git_commit\",   \"value\": \"<sha>\",             \"claim\": \"o selo est\u00e1 no hist\u00f3rico\" },\n#     { \"type\": \"file_exists\",  \"value\": \"docs/specs/x/05-sdd.md\", \"claim\": \"o desenho foi escrito\" },\n#     { \"type\": \"command_exit\", \"value\": \"<comando de probes.test.cmd>\", \"expect_exit\": 0,\n#                               \"claim\": \"a su\u00edte de testes passa\" },\n#     { \"type\": \"http_status\",  \"value\": \"https://exemplo/health\", \"expect_status\": 200,\n#                               \"claim\": \"o servi\u00e7o responde\" },\n#     { \"type\": \"record_id\",    \"value\": \"<id>\",              \"claim\": \"a observa\u00e7\u00e3o foi registrada\" }\n#   ]\n#\n#   `type` \u00e9 o que se sabe verificar; D\u00favida sobre a regra n\u00e3o pode virar leni\u00eancia silenciosa.\ndecide_strict() {\n  local blob\n  if [ -n \"$STRICT_OVERRIDE\" ]; then\n    STRICT=\"$STRICT_OVERRIDE\"\n    if [ \"$STRICT\" -eq 1 ]; then\n      STRICT_WHY='for\u00e7ado por --strict'\n    else\n      STRICT_WHY='RELAXADO por --no-strict \u2014 inverific\u00e1vel s\u00f3 avisa (decis\u00e3o sua, registre o porqu\u00ea)'\n    fi\n    return 0\n  fi\n  blob=\"$(cfg_list seal.sinks 2>/dev/null | tr '\\n' ' ' || true)\"\n  if [ -z \"${blob// /}\" ]; then\n    STRICT=1\n    STRICT_WHY='seal.sinks vazio ou ileg\u00edvel \u2014 assumindo ESTRITO (conservador por constru\u00e7\u00e3o)'\n    return 0\n  fi\n  if printf '%s' \"$blob\" | grep -qE '\"kind\"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*\"file\"'; then\n    STRICT=1\n    STRICT_WHY='seal.sinks inclui {kind:\"file\"}'\n    return 0\n  fi\n  STRICT=0\n  STRICT_WHY='seal.sinks n\u00e3o inclui {kind:\"file\"} \u2014 inverific\u00e1vel apenas avisa'\n  return 0\n}\n\n# \u2500\u2500 leitura do manifesto \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nread -r -d '' _PY <<'PYEOF' || true\nimport json, sys\ntry:\n    with open(sys.argv[1], 'r', encoding='utf-8') as fh:\n        doc = json.load(fh)\nexcept Exception as exc:\n    sys.stderr.write(\"proof-audit: manifesto invalido: %s\\n\" % exc)\n    sys.exit(2)\nitems = doc if isinstance(doc, list) else (doc.get('claims') if isinstance(doc, dict) else None)\nif not isinstance(items, list):\n    items = []\ndef clean(x):\n    return str(x).replace('\\t', ' ').replace('\\n', ' ')\nfor c in items:\n    if not isinstance(c, dict):\n        continue\n    e = c.get('expect_exit', c.get('expect_status', c.get('expect', '')))\n    e = '' if e is None else e\n    sys.stdout.write('\\t'.join([clean(c.get('type', '')), clean(c.get('value', '')),\n                                clean(e), clean(c.get('claim', ''))]) + '\\n')\nPYEOF\n\nread -r -d '' _JS <<'JSEOF' || true\nconst fs = require('fs');\nconst file = process.argv[1];\nlet doc;\ntry { doc = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8')); }\ncatch (e) { process.stderr.write('proof-audit: manifesto invalido: ' + e.message + '\\n'); process.exit(2); }\nlet items = Array.isArray(doc) ? doc : (doc && Array.isArray(doc.claims) ? doc.claims : []);\nconst clean = x => String(x === undefined || x === null ? '' : x).replace(/\\t/g, ' ').replace(/\\n/g, ' ');\nfor (const c of items) {\n  if (!c || typeof c !== 'object') continue;\n  let e = c.expect_exit;\n  if (e === undefined) e = c.expect_status;\n  if (e === undefined) e = c.expect;\n  process.stdout.write([clean(c.type), clean(c.value), clean(e), clean(c.claim)].join('\\t') + '\\n');\n}\nJSEOF\n\nread -r -d '' _JQ <<'JQEOF' || true\n(if type == \"array\" then . elif type == \"object\" then (.claims // []) else [] end)\n| .[]\n| select(type == \"object\")\n| [ (.type // \"\" | tostring),\n    (.value // \"\" | tostring),\n    ((if has(\"expect_exit\") then .expect_exit\n      elif has(\"expect_status\") then .expect_status\n      elif has(\"expect\") then .expect\n      else \"\" end) // \"\" | tostring),\n    (.claim // \"\" | tostring) ]\n| @tsv\nJQEOF\n\n# Fallback sem parser: fatiar por `}` e extrair campo a campo. N\u00e3o \u00e9 um parser JSON\n# e n\u00e3o finge ser \u2014 se ele devolver zero claims num arquivo que claramente tem\n# claims, o script sai INCONCLUSIVO (3) em vez de reportar verde.\nmanifest_fallback() {\n  local file=\"$1\" rec t v e c\n  tr '\\n' ' ' < \"$file\" | tr '}' '\\n' | while IFS= read -r rec; do\n    case \"$rec\" in *'\"type\"'*) ;; *) continue ;; esac\n    t=\"$(printf '%s' \"$rec\" | grep -oE '\"type\"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*\"[^\"]*\"' | head -n 1 \\\n         | sed -E 's/.*:[[:space:]]*\"//; s/\"$//')\"\n    v=\"$(printf '%s' \"$rec\" | grep -oE '\"value\"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*\"[^\"]*\"' | head -n 1 \\\n         | sed -E 's/.*:[[:space:]]*\"//; s/\"$//')\"\n    e=\"$(printf '%s' \"$rec\" | grep -oE '\"expect(_exit|_status)?\"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*\"?[^\",}]*\"?' | head -n 1 \\\n         | sed -E 's/.*:[[:space:]]*\"?//; s/\"$//; s/[[:space:]]+$//')\"\n    c=\"$(printf '%s' \"$rec\" | grep -oE '\"claim\"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*\"[^\"]*\"' | head -n 1 \\\n         | sed -E 's/.*:[[:space:]]*\"//; s/\"$//')\"\n    [ -n \"$t\" ] || continue\n    printf '%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\n' \"$t\" \"$v\" \"$e\" \"$c\"\n  done\n}\n\nmanifest_claims() {\n  local file=\"$1\" engine\n  engine=\"$(harness_json_engine)\"\n  case \"$engine\" in\n    jq)      jq -r \"$_JQ\" \"$file\" 2>/dev/null ;;\n    python3) python3 -c \"$_PY\" \"$file\" ;;\n    node)    node -e \"$_JS\" \"$file\" ;;\n    *)       manifest_fallback \"$file\" ;;\n  esac\n}\n\n# \u2500\u2500 verificadores \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n# Contrato de cada um: ecoa a EVID\u00caNCIA em EVIDENCE e retorna\n#   0 sustentada \u00b7 1 N\u00c3O sustentada \u00b7 2 N\u00c3O VERIFIC\u00c1VEL neste ambiente\nEVIDENCE=''\n\nv_git_commit() {\n  local val=\"$1\" t subj\n  if ! have git; then EVIDENCE='git n\u00e3o est\u00e1 no PATH'; return 2; fi\n  if ! git -C \"$ROOT\" rev-parse --is-inside-work-tree >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    EVIDENCE=\"n\u00e3o \u00e9 um reposit\u00f3rio git: $ROOT\"; return 2\n  fi\n  if [ -z \"$val\" ]; then EVIDENCE='claim sem `value`'; return 1; fi\n  t=\"$(git -C \"$ROOT\" cat-file -t \"$val\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  if [ \"$t\" != \"commit\" ]; then\n    EVIDENCE=\"git cat-file -t ${val} \u2192 '${t:-<objeto inexistente>}' (esperado: commit)\"\n    return 1\n  fi\n  subj=\"$(git -C \"$ROOT\" log -1 --format='%h %ad %s' --date=short \"$val\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  EVIDENCE=\"git cat-file -t \u2192 commit \u00b7 $subj\"\n  return 0\n}\n\nv_file_exists() {\n  local val=\"$1\" abs\n  if [ -z \"$val\" ]; then EVIDENCE='claim sem `value`'; return 1; fi\n  case \"$val\" in /*) abs=\"$val\" ;; *) abs=\"$ROOT/$val\" ;; esac\n  if [ ! -e \"$abs\" ]; then\n    EVIDENCE=\"n\u00e3o existe no disco: $val\"\n    return 1\n  fi\n  EVIDENCE=\"$(ls -la -- \"$abs\" 2>/dev/null | sed -e 's/[[:space:]]\\{1,\\}/ /g' | head -n 1)\"\n  return 0\n}\n\nv_command_exit() {\n  local val=\"$1\" expect=\"$2\" rc=0 log tail_txt\n  if [ \"$NO_EXEC\" -eq 1 ]; then\n    EVIDENCE='re-execu\u00e7\u00e3o desabilitada por --no-exec'\n    return 2\n  fi\n  if [ -z \"$val\" ]; then EVIDENCE='claim sem `value`'; return 1; fi\n  [ -n \"$expect\" ] || expect=0\n  case \"$expect\" in ''|*[!0-9-]*) expect=0 ;; esac\n  log=\"$(mktemp \"${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/harness-proof-cmd.XXXXXX\")\"\n  ( cd \"$ROOT\" && eval \"$val\" ) >\"$log\" 2>&1 || rc=$?\n  tail_txt=\"$(tail -n 2 \"$log\" 2>/dev/null | tr '\\n' ' ' | cut -c1-160)\"\n  rm -f \"$log\"\n  if [ \"$rc\" -eq \"$expect\" ]; then\n    EVIDENCE=\"re-executado agora \u2192 exit $rc (esperado $expect) \u00b7 $tail_txt\"\n    return 0\n  fi\n  EVIDENCE=\"re-executado agora \u2192 exit $rc, esperado $expect \u00b7 $tail_txt\"\n  return 1\n}\n\nv_http_status() {\n  local val=\"$1\" expect=\"$2\" code=''\n  [ -n \"$expect\" ] || expect=200\n  case \"$expect\" in ''|*[!0-9]*) expect=200 ;; esac\n  if [ -z \"$val\" ]; then EVIDENCE='claim sem `value`'; return 1; fi\n  if have curl; then\n    code=\"$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' --max-time \"$HTTP_TIMEOUT\" -- \"$val\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  elif have wget; then\n    code=\"$(wget --server-response --spider -T \"$HTTP_TIMEOUT\" -t 1 \"$val\" 2>&1 \\\n            | awk '/^[[:space:]]*HTTP\\//{ s=$2 } END{ print s }' || true)\"\n  else\n    EVIDENCE='nem curl nem wget no PATH'\n    return 2\n  fi\n  if [ -z \"$code\" ] || [ \"$code\" = \"000\" ]; then\n    EVIDENCE=\"requisi\u00e7\u00e3o n\u00e3o completou (timeout ${HTTP_TIMEOUT}s ou host inalcan\u00e7\u00e1vel)\"\n    return 1\n  fi\n  if [ \"$code\" = \"$expect\" ]; then\n    EVIDENCE=\"HTTP $code (esperado $expect)\"\n    return 0\n  fi\n  EVIDENCE=\"HTTP $code, esperado $expect\"\n  return 1\n}\n\nv_record_id() {\n  local val=\"$1\" adapter=\"$ROOT/.harness/bin/verify-record.sh\" rc=0 out\n  if [ ! -r \"$adapter\" ]; then\n    EVIDENCE='sem .harness/bin/verify-record.sh \u2014 o kit n\u00e3o conhece o seu armazenamento'\n    return 2\n  fi\n  if [ -z \"$val\" ]; then EVIDENCE='claim sem `value`'; return 1; fi\n  out=\"$(bash \"$adapter\" \"$val\" 2>&1)\" || rc=$?\n  case \"$rc\" in\n    0) EVIDENCE=\"verify-record.sh $val \u2192 existe \u00b7 $(printf '%s' \"$out\" | head -n 1 | cut -c1-120)\"; return 0 ;;\n    2) EVIDENCE=\"verify-record.sh declarou que n\u00e3o consegue verificar \u00b7 $(printf '%s' \"$out\" | head -n 1 | cut -c1-120)\"; return 2 ;;\n    *) EVIDENCE=\"verify-record.sh $val \u2192 exit $rc \u00b7 $(printf '%s' \"$out\" | head -n 1 | cut -c1-120)\"; return 1 ;;\n  esac\n}\n\n# \u2500\u2500 auditoria de um manifesto \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nN_OK=0; N_BAD=0; N_NV=0; N_CLAIMS=0\n\naudit_manifest() {\n  local file=\"$1\" tsv line type value expect claim rc\n  printf '\\n\u2500\u2500 %s \u2500\u2500\\n' \"${file#\"$ROOT\"/}\"\n\n  tsv=\"$(mktemp \"${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/harness-proof-tsv.XXXXXX\")\"\n  manifest_claims \"$file\" > \"$tsv\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\n  if [ ! -s \"$tsv\" ]; then\n    if grep -qE '\"type\"[[:space:]]*:' \"$file\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      rm -f \"$tsv\"\n      printf 'proof-audit: %s cont\u00e9m claims, mas a leitura devolveu ZERO.\\n' \"$file\" >&2\n      printf '             Auditoria INCONCLUSIVA \u2014 instale jq/python3/node.\\n' >&2\n      printf '             N\u00e3o vou reportar verde sobre um manifesto que n\u00e3o consegui ler.\\n' >&2\n      exit 3\n    fi\n    rm -f \"$tsv\"\n    printf '  manifesto sem nenhuma claim.\\n'\n    printf '  Um selo sem prova declarada n\u00e3o \u00e9 um selo verificado \u2014 \u00e9 um selo confiado.\\n'\n    N_BAD=$((N_BAD + 1))\n    return 0\n  fi\n\n  while IFS= read -r line; do\n    [ -n \"$line\" ] || continue\n    type=\"$(printf '%s' \"$line\" | cut -f1)\"\n    value=\"$(printf '%s' \"$line\" | cut -f2)\"\n    expect=\"$(printf '%s' \"$line\" | cut -f3)\"\n    claim=\"$(printf '%s' \"$line\" | cut -f4-)\"\n    N_CLAIMS=$((N_CLAIMS + 1))\n\n    EVIDENCE=''\n    rc=0\n    case \"$type\" in\n      git_commit)   v_git_commit   \"$value\" || rc=$? ;;\n      file_exists)  v_file_exists  \"$value\" || rc=$? ;;\n      command_exit) v_command_exit \"$value\" \"$expect\" || rc=$? ;;\n      http_status)  v_http_status  \"$value\" \"$expect\" || rc=$? ;;\n      record_id)    v_record_id    \"$value\" || rc=$? ;;\n      '')           rc=2; EVIDENCE='claim sem `type`' ;;\n      *)            rc=2; EVIDENCE=\"tipo desconhecido '$type' \u2014 nenhum verificador corresponde\" ;;\n    esac\n\n    case \"$rc\" in\n      0)\n        N_OK=$((N_OK + 1))\n        printf '  [ SUSTENTADA   ] %-13s %s\\n' \"$type\" \"${claim:-<sem afirma\u00e7\u00e3o>}\"\n        printf '                    prova: %s\\n' \"$EVIDENCE\"\n        ;;\n      1)\n        N_BAD=$((N_BAD + 1))\n        printf '  [ N\u00c3O SE SUSTENTA ] %-11s %s\\n' \"$type\" \"${claim:-<sem afirma\u00e7\u00e3o>}\"\n        printf '                    valor: %s\\n' \"${value:-<vazio>}\"\n        printf '                    achado: %s\\n' \"$EVIDENCE\"\n        ;;\n      *)\n        N_NV=$((N_NV + 1))\n        printf '  [ N\u00c3O VERIFIC\u00c1VEL ] %-11s %s\\n' \"$type\" \"${claim:-<sem afirma\u00e7\u00e3o>}\"\n        printf '                    valor: %s\\n' \"${value:-<vazio>}\"\n        printf '                    motivo: %s\\n' \"$EVIDENCE\"\n        if [ \"$STRICT\" -eq 1 ]; then\n          printf '                    \u2192 CONTA COMO FALHA (%s)\\n' \"$STRICT_WHY\"\n        else\n          printf '                    \u2192 apenas aviso (%s)\\n' \"$STRICT_WHY\"\n        fi\n        ;;\n    esac\n  done < \"$tsv\"\n  rm -f \"$tsv\"\n  return 0\n}\n\n# \u2500\u2500 descoberta do manifesto \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nprint_sample() {\n  cat <<'SAMPLE'\n\nMolde m\u00ednimo \u2014 grave em `<seal.proof_manifest>/<data>-<sess\u00e3o>.json`:\n\n[\n  { \"type\": \"git_commit\",   \"value\": \"<sha do selo>\",\n    \"claim\": \"o selo desta sess\u00e3o est\u00e1 no hist\u00f3rico\" },\n  { \"type\": \"file_exists\",  \"value\": \"<caminho relativo ao repo>\",\n    \"claim\": \"o artefato afirmado existe no disco\" },\n  { \"type\": \"command_exit\", \"value\": \"<comando declarado em probes.test.cmd>\", \"expect_exit\": 0,\n    \"claim\": \"a su\u00edte de testes passa\" }\n]\n\nRegra de ouro: uma claim por AFIRMA\u00c7\u00c3O feita no selo. Se a sess\u00e3o disse que o\nservi\u00e7o subiu, existe uma claim que prova que ele subiu \u2014 ou o selo n\u00e3o disse isso.\nSAMPLE\n}\n\nresolve_manifests() {\n  local base rel\n  if [ -n \"$MANIFEST_ARG\" ]; then\n    base=\"$MANIFEST_ARG\"\n  else\n    rel=\"$(cfg seal.proof_manifest '.harness/proof/')\"\n    [ -n \"$rel\" ] || rel='.harness/proof/'\n    case \"$rel\" in /*) base=\"$rel\" ;; *) base=\"$ROOT/$rel\" ;; esac\n  fi\n  base=\"${base%/}\"\n\n  if [ -f \"$base\" ]; then\n    printf '%s\\n' \"$base\"\n    return 0\n  fi\n  if [ -d \"$base\" ]; then\n    local found=''\n    if [ \"$ALL\" -eq 1 ]; then\n      found=\"$(find \"$base\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '*.json' 2>/dev/null | LC_ALL=C sort)\"\n    else\n      # Sem `ls -t` (formata\u00e7\u00e3o varia entre implementa\u00e7\u00f5es): ordena\u00e7\u00e3o lexicogr\u00e1fica\n      # do nome. Manifestos nomeados com data ISO ordenam corretamente por constru\u00e7\u00e3o.\n      found=\"$(find \"$base\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '*.json' 2>/dev/null | LC_ALL=C sort | tail -n 1)\"\n    fi\n    [ -n \"$found\" ] && printf '%s\\n' \"$found\"\n    return 0\n  fi\n  return 0\n}\n\n# \u2500\u2500 selftest \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nSELFTEST_ROOT=''\nselftest_cleanup() {\n  [ -n \"${SELFTEST_ROOT:-}\" ] && [ -d \"${SELFTEST_ROOT:-}\" ] && rm -rf \"$SELFTEST_ROOT\"\n  return 0\n}\n\ncmd_selftest() {\n  local fails=0 t rc sha\n  if ! have git; then\n    printf 'proof-audit --selftest: git indispon\u00edvel \u2014 n\u00e3o consigo montar a \u00e1rvore de prova.\\n' >&2\n    return 9\n  fi\n  SELFTEST_ROOT=\"$(mktemp -d \"${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/harness-proof-selftest.XXXXXX\")\"\n  trap selftest_cleanup EXIT\n\n  _a() {\n    local desc=\"$1\"; shift\n    if \"$@\"; then printf '  [PASS] %s\\n' \"$desc\"; return 0; fi\n    printf '  [FAIL] %s\\n' \"$desc\"; fails=$((fails + 1)); return 1\n  }\n\n  t=\"$SELFTEST_ROOT/repo\"\n  mkdir -p \"$t/.harness/bin\" \"$t/.harness/lib\" \"$t/.harness/proof\" \"$t/docs\"\n  cp -- \"$SELF\" \"$t/.harness/bin/proof-audit.sh\"\n  cp -- \"$LIB\"  \"$t/.harness/lib/harness-lib.sh\"\n  cat > \"$t/.harness/harness.config.json\" <<'JSON'\n{\n  \"kit_version\": \"1.0.0\",\n  \"project\": { \"name\": \"selftest\", \"slug\": \"selftest\", \"response_language\": \"pt-BR\", \"code_language\": \"en\" },\n  \"roles\": { \"operator\": \"AI Agent\", \"reviewer\": \"selftest\", \"owner\": \"selftest\", \"escalation\": \"\" },\n  \"paths\": {\n    \"specs\": \"docs/specs\",\n    \"processes\": \"docs/processes\",\n    \"architecture\": \"docs/architecture\",\n    \"handoff\": \"HANDOFF.md\",\n    \"archive\": \"docs/handoff-archive\",\n    \"knowledge\": \"docs/knowledge\"\n  },\n  \"seal\": { \"commit_convention\": \"conventional\", \"coauthor_trailer\": false,\n            \"proof_manifest\": \".harness/proof/\", \"archive_after_records\": 12,\n            \"sinks\": [{ \"kind\": \"file\" }] },\n  \"knowledge\": { \"backend\": \"file\", \"sink\": \"docs/knowledge/observations.md\" }\n}\nJSON\n  printf '# artefato real\\n' > \"$t/docs/existe.md\"\n  (\n    cd \"$t\"\n    git init -q .\n    git -c user.email=selftest@harness -c user.name=selftest add -A\n    git -c user.email=selftest@harness -c user.name=selftest commit -q -m 'baseline'\n  ) >/dev/null 2>&1\n  sha=\"$( cd \"$t\" && git rev-parse HEAD 2>/dev/null || true )\"\n  if [ -z \"$sha\" ]; then\n    printf '  [FAIL] n\u00e3o consegui criar um commit real na \u00e1rvore sint\u00e9tica\\n' >&2\n    return 9\n  fi\n\n  _run() { # _run <manifesto-relativo> [flags\u2026]\n    local m=\"$1\"; shift\n    ( cd \"$t\" && HARNESS_CONFIG=\"$t/.harness/harness.config.json\" \\\n        bash .harness/bin/proof-audit.sh \"$m\" \"$@\" ) > \"$SELFTEST_ROOT/out\" 2>&1\n    printf '%s' \"$?\"\n  }\n\n  printf 'proof-audit --selftest \u00b7 \u00e1rvore sint\u00e9tica em %s\\n' \"$t\"\n  printf 'commit real gerado: %s\\n\\n' \"$sha\"\n\n  printf 'A. manifesto ausente \u2014 aus\u00eancia de prova n\u00e3o \u00e9 prova\\n'\n  rc=\"$(_run '.harness/proof/inexistente.json')\"\n  _a 'exit 1' test \"$rc\" -eq 1\n\n  printf '\\nB. manifesto misto: 3 sustentadas \u00b7 3 falsas \u00b7 1 inverific\u00e1vel (estrito)\\n'\n  cat > \"$t/.harness/proof/misto.json\" <<JSON\n[\n  { \"type\": \"git_commit\",   \"value\": \"$sha\", \"claim\": \"o selo est\u00e1 no hist\u00f3rico\" },\n  { \"type\": \"git_commit\",   \"value\": \"abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01\", \"claim\": \"commit inventado\" },\n  { \"type\": \"file_exists\",  \"value\": \"docs/existe.md\", \"claim\": \"o artefato existe\" },\n  { \"type\": \"file_exists\",  \"value\": \"docs/nunca-escrito.md\", \"claim\": \"arquivo que o selo afirmou\" },\n  { \"type\": \"command_exit\", \"value\": \"true\",  \"expect_exit\": 0, \"claim\": \"o comando passa\" },\n  { \"type\": \"command_exit\", \"value\": \"false\", \"expect_exit\": 0, \"claim\": \"o comando que falha\" },\n  { \"type\": \"record_id\",    \"value\": \"42\", \"claim\": \"registro sem verificador neste ambiente\" }\n]\nJSON\n  rc=\"$(_run '.harness/proof/misto.json')\"\n  _a 'exit 4 (3 falsas + 1 inverific\u00e1vel em modo estrito)' test \"$rc\" -eq 4\n  _a 'a inverific\u00e1vel foi nomeada como tal' grep -q 'N\u00c3O VERIFIC\u00c1VEL' \"$SELFTEST_ROOT/out\"\n  _a 'e declarada como falha' ## Verificações independentes
- **Auto-imunidade provada:** kit copiado para fora do repo → `secret-scan --all` = `0 credenciais em 0 linha(s) candidata(s)`, exit 0. (Rodado dentro do repo hospedeiro ele acha 21 ocorrências reais/estruturais — o scanner funciona sobre código de verdade.)
- **proof-audit cross-engine:** selftest verde forçando `HARNESS_JSON_ENGINE` em `jq`, `python3`, `node` **e o fallback `awk`**.
- **E2E real:** install num repo git novo com `CLAUDE.md` pré-existente → texto do time preservado, bloco no topo, `.gitignore` com as 2 linhas, hooks instalados (`core.hooksPath = .harness/githooks`), `proof-audit` sem manifesto → "NENHUMA PROVA APRESENTADA" exit 1.
- **`build.sh` preflight:** `caminhos absolutos ok · sintaxe dos scripts ok · harness.config.json válido ok · canário ok` → zip empacotado (24 arquivos).

## Dois defeitos que eu mesmo introduzi e corrigi (achados pelo E2E, não pela leitura)
1. `README.md` dentro de `paths.processes` fazia o `doctor.sh` §6 reportar **"1 processo documentado"** sobre zero processos reais — falso-verde da mesma família que o kit existe para matar. Corrigido: o README de diretório é **sem extensão**, com o motivo comentado no código. Agora o doctor diz honestamente `0 processos documentados`.
2. `docs/knowledge/observations.md` é citado por `.claude/rules/01-closed-loop.md` §8 e não era criado → **ponteiro morto** no §4 do doctor. Corrigido: o instalador semeia o sink quando `knowledge.backend = file`. §4 agora: `1 citação(ões) verificada(s), todas resolvem`.

Depois das correções, o doctor pós-instalação sai **5 FAIL**, e os cinco são exatamente os campos da Fase B/D ainda em branco (`project.name`, `project.slug`, `roles.reviewer`, `roles.owner`, `spec_gate.trigger_paths`) — falha esperada e documentada no cabeçalho do `install.sh`.

## Decisões de contrato que valem registro
- **`secrets.engines` externo** não é invocado por comando presumido: exige adaptador `.harness/bin/secret-engine-<nome>.sh` do projeto; sem ele o motor é reportado **NÃO EXECUTADO**, nunca verde (Proibição 3).
- **`proof-audit` é estrito por construção:** se `seal.sinks` for ilegível, assume estrito e diz que assumiu. `record_id` sem `.harness/bin/verify-record.sh` → NÃO VERIFICÁVEL → **falha**. Provado no selftest (exit 4 estrito · exit 3 com `--no-strict` · exit 3 com adaptador presente).
- **Falso-positivo declarado no cabeçalho do scanner:** `"fake-test-only-token"` num teste **é** flagrado, de propósito — `test`/`fake` nunca absolvem. A saída correta é escrever placeholder, não silenciar.
- `harness-kit/dist/` (zip do build de verificação) já é coberto pela regra `dist` do `.gitignore` do repo.
